oA,oD=0,1 while oE>0 or oz>0 do oA=oA+oP[((oE%0x10))*0x10+oz%16]*oD oE,oz,oD=ki(oE/16),ki(oz/16),oD*0b10000 end return oA end kU=function(oQ,oo)local
